Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electrochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 393 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 903 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 393 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrochemistry structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 903. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
